Abstract: Social-educational robots designed for socially interactive pedagogical support, such as the Robot Study Companion (RSC), rely on responsive, privacy-preserving interaction despite severely limited compute. However, there is a gap in systematic benchmarking of language models for edge computing in pedagogical applications. This paper benchmarks 25 open-source language models for local deployment on edge hardware. We evaluate each model across three dimensions: inference efficiency (tokens per second, energy consumption), general knowledge (a six-category MMLU subset), and teaching effectiveness (LLM-rated pedagogical quality), validated against five independent human raters using the Raspberry Pi(RPi)4 as the primary platform, with additional comparisons on the RPi5 and a laptop GPU.
  Results reveal pronounced trade-offs: throughput and energy efficiency vary by over an order of magnitude across models, MMLU accuracy ranges from near-random to 57.2%, and teaching effectiveness does not correlate monotonically with either metric. Among the evaluated models, Granite4 Tiny Hybrid (7B) achieves a strong overall balance, reaching 2.5 tokens per second, 0.90 tokens per joule, and 54.6% MMLU accuracy; high MMLU accuracy does not appear necessary for strong teaching scores. Human validation on four representative models preserved the automated rank ordering (Pearson r = 0.967, n = 4). Based on these findings, we propose a three-tier local inference architecture for the RSC that balances responsiveness and accuracy on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [95] [Robust Path Tracking for Vehicles via Continuous-Time Residual Learning: An ICODE-MPPI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03260)
*Shugen Song,Wenjie Mei,Chengyan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于输入伴随神经常微分方程（ICODE）的鲁棒模型预测路径积分控制框架（ICODE-MPPI），用于提升非线性系统在未建模动态和持续干扰下的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPPI控制受限于名义动力学模型的精度，难以应对未建模动态和持续扰动，导致跟踪误差和控制抖振问题。

Method: 引入ICODE学习并补偿系统中的残差动力学，利用其连续时间特性保持物理一致性和时间连续性，并嵌入MPPI框架中实现高保真预测控制。

Result: 在复杂轨迹的高保真仿真中，相比标准MPPI，ICODE-MPPI实现了最高达69%的横向跟踪误差降低，并显著抑制了控制抖振，提升了控制平滑性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ICODE-MPPI通过融合连续时间学习模型与采样式最优控制，有效提升了非线性系统的控制精度与鲁棒性，适用于高动态复杂环境下的自主控制任务。

Abstract: Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is a powerful sampling-based strategy for nonlinear autonomous systems. However, its performance is often bottlenecked by the fidelity of nominal dynamics. We propose ICODE-MPPI, a robust framework that leverages Input Concomitant Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ICODEs) to learn and compensate for unmodeled residual dynamics. Unlike discrete-time learners, ICODEs maintain physical consistency and temporal continuity during the MPPI prediction horizon. High-fidelity simulations on complex trajectories demonstrate that ICODE-MPPI achieves up to a 69\% reduction in cross-tracking error under persistent disturbances compared to standard MPPI control. Furthermore, our analysis confirms that ICODE-MPPI significantly suppresses control chattering, yielding smoother steering commands and superior robust performance.

</details>


### [96] [RLDX-1 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03269)
*Dongyoung Kim,Huiwon Jang,Myungkyu Koo,Suhyeok Jang,Taeyoung Kim,Beomjun Kim,Byungjun Yoon,Changsung Jang,Daewon Choi,Dongsu Han,Donguk Lee,Heeseung Kwon,Hojin Jeon,Jaehyun Kang,Jaekyoung Bae,Jihyuk Lee,Jimin Lee,John Won,Joonwoo Ahn,Junhyeong Park,Junyoung Sung,Kyungmin Lee,Minseong Han,Minsung Yoon,Sejune Joo,Seonil Son,Seungcheol Park,Seunggeun Cho,Seungjun Moon,Seungku Kim,Yonghoon Dong,Yongjin Cho,Youngchan Kim,Chang Hwan Kim,Dohyeon Kim,Hazel Lee,Heecheol Kim,Hensen Ahn,Hyungkyu Ryu,Hyunsoo Choi,Hyunsoo Shin,Jaeheon Jung,Jaewoo Kim,Jinwook Kim,Joochul Chang,Joonsoo Kim,Junghun Park,Jungwoo Park,Junho Cho,Junhyeok Park,Junwon Lee,Kangwook Lee,Kwanghoon Kim,Kyoungwhan Choe,Manoj Bhadu,Nayoung Oh,Sangjun Kim,Sangwoo Kim,Seunghoon Shim,Seunghyun Kim,Seungjun Lee,Seungyup Ka,Sungryol Yang,Wook Jung,Yashu Shukla,Yeonjae Lee,Yeonwoo Bae,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RLDX-1是一种基于多流动作变换器（MSAT）的通用机器人策略，通过整合多种模态信息，在复杂灵巧操作任务中显著优于现有视觉-语言-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型在需要运动感知、记忆决策和物理感知等多功能能力的复杂现实任务中仍存在困难。

Method: 提出RLDX-1，采用多流动作变换器架构，结合模态特定流与跨模态联合自注意力机制，并引入稀有场景训练数据合成、类人操作专用学习流程和实时推理优化等系统级设计。

Result: 在模拟基准和真实世界任务中，RLDX-1均优于前沿模型（如$π_{0.5}$和GR00T N1.6），在ALLEX人形任务中达到86.8%的成功率，远超后者的约40%。

Conclusion: RLDX-1通过统一异构模态和系统级优化，成为迈向可靠、适用于复杂动态现实环境的视觉-语言-动作模型的重要一步。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs) have shown remarkable progress toward human-like generalist robotic policies through the versatile intelligence (i.e. broad scene understanding and language-conditioned generalization) inherited from pre-trained Vision-Language Models, they still struggle with complex real-world tasks requiring broader functional capabilities (e.g. motion awareness, memory-aware decision making, and physical sensing). To address this, we introduce RLDX-1, a general-purpose robotic policy for dexterous manipulation built on the Multi-Stream Action Transformer (MSAT), an architecture that unifies these capabilities by integrating heterogeneous modalities through modality-specific streams with cross-modal joint self-attention. RLDX-1 further combines this architecture with system-level design choices, including synthesizing training data for rare manipulation scenarios, learning procedures specialized for human-like manipulation, and inference optimizations for real-time deployment. Through empirical evaluation, we show that RLDX-1 consistently outperforms recent frontier VLAs (e.g. $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.6) across both simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks that require broad functional capabilities beyond general versatility. In particular, RLDX-1 shows superiority in ALLEX humanoid tasks by achieving success rates of 86.8% while $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.6 achieve around 40%, highlighting the ability of RLDX-1 to control a high-DoF humanoid robot under diverse functional demands. Together, these results position RLDX-1 as a promising step toward reliable VLAs for complex, contact-rich, and dynamic real-world dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [97] [Height Control and Optimal Torque Planning for Jumping With Wheeled-Bipedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03302)
*Yulun Zhuang,Yuan Xu,Binxin Huang,Mandan Chao,Guowei Shi,Xin Yang,Kuangen Zhang,Chenglong Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扭矩规划和能量优化的轮式双足机器人精确跳跃高度控制方法，结合W-JBD动力学模型与BOTP扭矩优化算法，显著降低高度误差与能耗，并在仿真中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于轮式双足机器人在跳跃过程中存在欠驱动、非线性估计和瞬时冲击等问题，导致跳跃高度难以精确控制，且常因过度跳跃造成能量浪费和高地面反作用力。

Method: 提出了一种新型轮式双足跳跃动力学模型（W-JBD），并结合贝叶斯优化扭矩规划方法（BOTP）实现无需精确动力学模型的最优扭矩规划，通过Webots仿真平台进行验证。

Result: BOTP方法使跳跃高度误差减少82.3%，能量消耗降低26.9%，并生成连续扭矩曲线；结合W-JBD模型可将搜索空间缩小，平均仅需40次迭代即可收敛。

Conclusion: 结合W-JBD模型与BOTP方法，可在较少实验次数下实现真实机器人跳跃高度的精确控制，兼顾效率与可行性。

Abstract: This paper mainly studies the accurate height jumping control of wheeled-bipedal robots based on torque planning and energy consumption optimization. Due to the characteristics of underactuated, nonlinear estimation, and instantaneous impact in the jumping process, accurate control of the wheeled-bipedal robot's jumping height is complicated. In reality, robots often jump at excessive height to ensure safety, causing additional motor loss, greater ground reaction force and more energy consumption. To solve this problem, a novel wheeled-bipedal jumping dynamical model(W-JBD) is proposed to achieve accurate height control. It performs well but not suitable for the real robot because the torque has a striking step. Therefore, the Bayesian optimization for torque planning method(BOTP) is proposed, which can obtain the optimal torque planning without accurate dynamic model and within few iterations. BOTP method can reduce 82.3% height error, 26.9% energy cost with continuous torque curve. This result is validated in the Webots simulation platform. Based on the torque curve obtained in the W-JBD model to narrow the searching space, BOTP can quickly converge (40 times on average). Cooperating W-JBD model and BOTP method, it is possible to achieve the height control of real robots with reasonable times of experiments.

</details>


### [98] [Learning Reactive Dexterous Grasping via Hierarchical Task-Space RL Planning and Joint-Space QP Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03363)
*Ho Jae Lee,Yonghyeon Lee,Alexander Alexiev,Tzu-Yuan Lin,Se Hwan Jeon,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合分层控制框架，用于反应式灵巧抓取，通过解耦高层空间意图与底层关节执行，实现安全、可扩展且具备零样本迁移能力的灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灵巧抓取方法难以兼顾实时反应性、硬件安全性和对未见环境的泛化能力，尤其是在动态干扰和未知障碍下。因此需要一种能够分离决策层级、保障安全并支持在线调整的控制架构。

Method: 设计了一个基于多智能体强化学习的分层控制框架，其中臂部和手部分别由独立的智能体负责生成任务空间速度指令；这些指令由GPU并行化的二次规划（QP）控制器转化为满足运动学约束和避障要求的关节速度。

Result: 在仿真到现实的严格验证流程中表现出色，真实实验中使用7自由度机械臂和20自由度仿人手成功实现了对多种未见物体的鲁棒零样本迁移抓取，并能在非结构化环境中主动应对突发物理扰动。

Conclusion: 该框架通过明确的层级解耦提升了训练效率和安全性，同时实现了无需重新训练的动态可调节性与零样本转移能力，为复杂环境下的灵巧操作提供了实用且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a hybrid hierarchical control framework for reactive dexterous grasping that explicitly decouples high-level spatial intent from low-level joint execution. We introduce a multi-agent reinforcement learning architecture, specialized into distinct arm and hand agents, that acts as a high-level planner by generating desired task-space velocity commands. These commands are then processed by a GPU-parallelized quadratic programming controller, which translates them into feasible joint velocities while strictly enforcing kinematic limits and collision avoidance. This structural isolation not only accelerates training convergence but also strictly enforces hardware safety. Furthermore, the architecture unlocks zero-shot steerability, allowing system operators to dynamically adjust safety margins and avoid dynamic obstacles without retraining the policy. We extensively validate the proposed framework through a rigorous simulation-to-reality pipeline. Real-world hardware experiments on a 7-DoF arm equipped with a 20-DoF anthropomorphic hand demonstrate highly robust zero-shot transferability for dexterous grasping to a diverse set of unseen objects, highlighting the system's ability to reactively recover from unexpected physical disturbances in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [99] [Bridging the Embodiment Gap: Disentangled Cross-Embodiment Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03637)
*Zhiyuan Li,Wenyan Yang,Wenshuai Zhao,Yue Ma,Yuanpeng Tu,Pekka Marttinen,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种生成式框架，通过解耦任务和具身表征，实现从人类演示视频到机器人执行视频的跨具身视频编辑，无需配对数据即可生成时序一致且形态准确的机器人操作视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从人类视频学习机器人操作时因人机具身差异导致的分布偏移问题，避免现有方法中任务相关信息与人类特有运动学纠缠的问题。

Method: 设计双对比目标，将演示视频分解为两个正交的潜在空间（任务与具身），最小化两者间互信息以确保独立性，同时最大化空间内一致性；使用参数高效适配器将潜在码注入冻结的视频扩散模型，实现单一人类演示生成机器人执行视频。

Result: 实验表明该方法能生成时序连贯、形态准确的机器人演示视频，且无需配对的跨具身训练数据，在利用互联网规模的人类视频进行机器人学习方面具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过显式解耦任务与具身表征，有效克服了跨具身迁移中的表示纠缠问题，为利用大规模人类视频数据训练机器人提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Learning robotic manipulation from human videos is a promising solution to the data bottleneck in robotics, but the distribution shift between humans and robots remains a critical challenge. Existing approaches often produce entangled representations, where task-relevant information is coupled with human-specific kinematics, limiting their adaptability. We propose a generative framework for cross-embodiment video editing that directly addresses this by learning explicitly disentangled task and embodiment representations. Our method factorizes a demonstration video into two orthogonal latent spaces by enforcing a dual contrastive objective: it minimizes mutual information between the spaces to ensure independence while maximizing intra-space consistency to create stable representations. A parameter-efficient adapter injects these latent codes into a frozen video diffusion model, enabling the synthesis of a coherent robot execution video from a single human demonstration, without requiring paired cross-embodiment data. Experiments show our approach generates temporally consistent and morphologically accurate robot demonstrations, offering a scalable solution to leverage internet-scale human video for robot learning.

</details>


### [100] [Jiao: Bridging Isolation and Customization in Mixed Criticality Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03641)
*James Yen,Zhibai Huang,Zhixiang Wei,Tinghao Yi,Shupeng Zeng,Liang Pang,Songtao Xue,Zhengwei Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向消费机器人共享多核平台的安全架构，通过三个组件解决用户与平台开发者之间的知识不对称问题，并在ARM Cortex-A55上验证了其显著降低时序抖动和尾部误差的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费机器人需要在共享多核平台上整合安全关键控制、感知流水线和用户应用，但终端用户缺乏系统知识，导致难以直接移植汽车领域的静态分区架构，存在专家知识不对称问题。

Method: 设计了一个包含三个组件的架构：Safe IO Cell提供硬件级覆盖能力，Parameter Synchronization Service封装跨域复杂性，Safety Communication Layer实现符合IEC 61508标准的验证机制。

Result: 在ARM Cortex-A55平台上的实验表明，该架构使周期抖动减少了84.5%，尾部时序误差（p99 |jitter|）从69.0μs降至7.8μs，完全消除了超过50μs的异常。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构有效解决了消费机器人中用户行为修改与系统安全性之间的矛盾，在保证强隔离的同时降低了用户使用门槛，提升了实时性和安全性。

Abstract: Consumer robotics demands consolidation of safety-critical control, perception pipelines, and user applications on shared multicore platforms. While static partitioning hypervisors provide hardware-enforced isolation, directly transplanting automotive architectures encounters an expertise asymmetry problem in which end-users modifying robot behavior lack the systems knowledge that platform developers possess. We present an architecture addressing this challenge through three integrated components. A Safe IO Cell provides hardware-level override capability. A Parameter Synchronization Service encapsulates cross-domain complexity. A Safety Communication Layer implements IEC~61508-aligned verification. Our empirical evaluation on an ARM Cortex-A55 platform demonstrates that partition isolation reduces cycle-period jitter by 84.5\% and cuts tail timing error by nearly an order of magnitude (p99 $|$jitter$|$ from 69.0\,$μ$s to 7.8\,$μ$s), eliminating all $>$50\,$μ$s~excursions.

</details>


### [101] [Feasibility-aware Hybrid Control for Motion Planning under Signal Temporal Logics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03662)
*Panagiotis Rousseas,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合建模的平面任务与运动规划新方法，通过离散变量和控制屏障函数解决非凸工作空间中的死锁问题，并在仿真中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统任务与运动规划中因几何复杂和非凸机器人工作空间导致的规划困难及死锁问题，需要一种能够统一规划与控制设计的方法。

Method: 引入一个用于建模局部约束满足并实现局部可行性分析的离散变量，结合在转换后的圆形版本工作空间上设计的控制屏障函数，实现任务与运动的协同规划。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能有效处理多个重叠的时空任务，即使在输入饱和的情况下也能保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合建模范式成功地将高层任务规划与底层控制相结合，提升了复杂环境下的机器人自主性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: In this work, a novel method for planar task and motion planning based on hybrid modeling is proposed. By virtue of a discrete variable which models local constraint satisfaction and enables local feasibility analysis, the proposed control architecture unifies planning with control design. Concurrently, control barrier functions are designed on a transformed disk version of the original nonconvex and geometrically complex robotic workspace, thus amending the issue of deadlocks. Simulations of the proposed method indicate effective handling of multiple overlapping spatio-temporal tasks even in the face of input saturation.

</details>


### [102] [Sensorless State Estimation and Control for Agile Cable-Suspended Payload Transport by Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03666)
*Ana Maria Nascimento,Augusto Sales,Antonio Marcus Lima,Tiago Nascimento*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Udwadia-Kalaba方法的无人机空中操纵电缆悬挂负载的新控制与估计方法，通过显式引入几何约束实现了张力预测与无传感器状态估计，并在真实实验中验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖直接测量和拉格朗日建模，缺乏完整动态模型导致性能受限，因此需要一种不依赖直接测量且能准确描述系统约束的新方法。

Method: 采用Udwadia-Kalaba方法显式引入缆绳几何约束，推导出张力并集成到非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）中；同时提出基于相同几何约束的无传感器负载状态估计方法。

Result: 真实机器人实验结果表明，将负载动力学显式纳入优化问题可显著减少轨迹跟踪误差，相比基于不完整模型的策略具有更优的整体性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过合理利用几何约束，在无需直接测量的情况下实现了高精度的负载操控与状态估计，为无人机空中操作提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel control and estimation approach for aerial manipulation of a cable-suspended load using Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs). Common approaches in the state of the art have practical limitations, relying on direct load measurements and Lagrangian methods for dynamic modeling. The lack of a straightforward dynamic model of the system led us to propose adopting the Udwadia-Kalaba method to explicitly incorporate the cable's geometric constraints. This formulation allowed for the consistent derivation of the tension force and its direct integration into the NMPC prediction model. Additionally, we propose a sensorless load state estimation based on the same geometric constraints. Results from real-robot experiments demonstrated that the explicit inclusion of load dynamics in the optimization problem significantly reduces trajectory-tracking errors and yields better overall performance compared to strategies based on incomplete models.

</details>


### [103] [FUS3DMaps: Scalable and Accurate Open-Vocabulary Semantic Mapping by 3D Fusion of Voxel- and Instance-Level Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03669)
*Timon Homberger,Finn Lukas Busch,Jesús Gerardo Ortega Peimbert,Quantao Yang,Olov Andersson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FUS3DMaps是一种在线双层语义建图方法，结合密集层和实例层的开放词汇语义信息，在共享体素地图中实现跨层融合，提升建图质量与可扩展性，支持多楼层建筑规模的高精度开放词汇语义建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需训练的开放词汇语义建图方法在可扩展性上受限，尤其是基于图像块投影的密集建图方法虽避免了分割与2D-3D关联难题，但难以兼顾实例级建图的准确性与效率。

Method: 提出FUS3DMaps，构建一个共享体素地图中的双层语义建图框架，同时维护密集层和实例层，并通过体素级别的跨层语义融合机制整合两者优势；采用空间滑动窗口限制计算范围，提升系统可扩展性与实时性。

Result: 在多个3D语义分割基准和大规模场景上实验表明，FUS3DMaps在多楼层建筑尺度下实现了高精度的开放词汇语义建图，且显著提升了实例层与密集层的语义质量。

Conclusion: FUS3DMaps通过双层协同建模与跨层融合机制，有效结合了密集建图与实例建图的优势，在保证可扩展性的同时实现了高质量、准确的开放词汇语义地图构建，适用于大尺度真实环境。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic mapping enables robots to spatially ground previously unseen concepts without requiring predefined class sets. Current training-free methods commonly rely on multi-view fusion of semantic embeddings into a 3D map, either at the instance-level via segmenting views and encoding image crops of segments, or by projecting image patch embeddings directly into a dense semantic map. The latter approach sidesteps segmentation and 2D-to-3D instance association by operating on full uncropped image frames, but existing methods remain limited in scalability. We present FUS3DMaps, an online dual-layer semantic mapping method that jointly maintains both dense and instance-level open-vocabulary layers within a shared voxel map. This design enables further voxel-level semantic fusion of the layer embeddings, combining the complementary strengths of both semantic mapping approaches. We find that our proposed semantic cross-layer fusion approach improves the quality of both the instance-level and dense layers, while also enabling a scalable and highly accurate instance-level map where the dense layer and cross-layer fusion are restricted to a spatial sliding window. Experiments on established 3D semantic segmentation benchmarks as well as a selection of large-scale scenes show that FUS3DMaps achieves accurate open-vocabulary semantic mapping at multi-story building scales. Additional material and code will be made available: https://githanonymous.github.io/FUS3DMaps/.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Visual SLAM for UAV Navigation in GPS-Denied and Degraded Environments: A Multi-Paradigm Evaluation and Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03678)
*Prasoon Kumar,Akshay Deepak,Sandeep Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种V-SLAM系统在GPS拒绝和视觉退化环境下的性能，涵盖传统与深度学习方法，实验基于多个公开和自建数据集，在多种退化条件下进行评估，并提供适用于无人机嵌入式平台的选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS失效且视觉条件恶劣的环境中实现可靠的无人机定位至关重要，现有SLAM系统在极端退化条件下的鲁棒性和适用性尚不明确，需系统性评估以指导实际应用。

Method: 对ORB-SLAM3、DPVO、DROID-SLAM、DUSt3R和MASt3R五种V-SLAM系统进行对比实验，使用TUM RGB-D、EuRoC MAV、UMA-VI、SubT-MRS四个公开基准和一个自建单目室内数据集，在正常、低光、尘雾、运动模糊及复合退化条件下测试，采用亚毫米级Vicon真值进行精度评估，并在NVIDIA Jetson平台分析嵌入式部署性能。

Result: ORB-SLAM3在严重退化下表现差（总体TSR 62.4%，浓雾下为0%），而基于学习的方法更鲁棒：MASt3R取得最低退化ATE（0.027 m），DUSt3R跟踪成功率最高（96.5%）；DPVO在效率与鲁棒性间平衡最佳（18.6 FPS，3.1 GB GPU内存，86.1% TSR）。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法在视觉退化环境下显著优于传统SLAM，其中DPVO最适合资源受限的无人机嵌入式平台，研究结果为SWaP约束下的SLAM系统选型提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Reliable localization in GPS-denied, visually degraded environments is critical for autonomous UAV opera- tions. This paper presents a systematic comparative evaluation of five V-SLAM systems ORB-SLAM3, DPVO, DROID-SLAM, DUSt3R, and MASt3R spanning classical, deep learning, recurrent, and Vision Transformer (ViT) paradigms. Experiments are conducted on curated sequences from four public benchmarks (TUM RGB-D, EuRoC MAV, UMA-VI, SubT-MRS) and a custom monocular indoor dataset under five controlled degradation conditions (normal, low light, dust haze, motion blur, and combined), with sub-millimeter Vicon ground truth. Results show that ORB-SLAM3 fails critically under severe degradation (62.4% overall TSR; 0% under dense haze), while learning-based methods remain robust: MASt3R achieves the lowest degraded ATE (0.027 m) and DUSt3R the highest tracking success (96.5%). DPVO offers the best efficiency robustness trade-off (18.6 FPS, 3.1 GB GPU memory, 86.1% TSR), making it the preferred choice for memory-constrained embedded platforms. Embedded deployment analysis across NVIDIA Jetson platforms provides actionable guidelines for SLAM selection under SWaP-constrained UAV scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [RoboAlign-R1: Distilled Multimodal Reward Alignment for Robot Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03821)
*Hao Wu,Yuqi Li,Yuan Gao,Fan Xu,Fan Zhang,Kun Wang,Penghao Zhao,Qiufeng Wang,Yizhou Zhao,Weiyan Wang,Yingli Tian,Xian Wu,Xiaomeng Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAlign-R1 是一种结合奖励对齐后训练与稳定长视野推理的机器人视频世界模型框架，通过构建大规模基准和教师-学生奖励模型提升任务一致性、物理真实性和长序列预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人视频世界模型依赖低层次训练目标（如重构误差），与实际决策能力（如指令跟随、操作成功率）脱节，且在长序列自回归预测中易积累误差。

Method: 提出 RoboAlign-R1 框架：1）构建包含 10,000 个视频-指令对的 RobotWorldBench 基准；2）训练多模态教师判别模型 RoboAlign-Judge 进行六维细粒度评估，并蒸馏为轻量学生奖励模型用于强化学习后训练；3）引入无需训练的滑动窗口重编码（SWR）策略，周期性刷新生成上下文以减少长视野漂移。

Result: 在领域内评估中，RoboAlign-R1 相比最强基线六维综合得分提升 10.1%，其中操作准确率提升 7.5%，指令跟随提升 4.6%；SWR 在仅增加约 1% 延迟下，SSIM 提升 2.8%，LPIPS 降低 9.8%；结果经外部视觉语言模型交叉验证和盲人研究支持。

Conclusion: 奖励对齐的后训练与稳定的长视野解码能有效提升机器人视频世界模型的任务一致性、物理合理性和长序列预测性能。

Abstract: Existing robot video world models are typically trained with low-level objectives such as reconstruction and perceptual similarity, which are poorly aligned with the capabilities that matter most for robot decision making, including instruction following, manipulation success, and physical plausibility. They also suffer from error accumulation in long-horizon autoregressive prediction. We present RoboAlign-R1, a framework that combines reward-aligned post-training with stabilized long-horizon inference for robot video world models. We construct RobotWorldBench, a benchmark of 10,000 annotated video-instruction pairs collected from four robot data sources, and train a multimodal teacher judge, RoboAlign-Judge, to provide fine-grained six-dimensional evaluation of generated videos. We then distill the teacher into a lightweight student reward model for efficient reinforcement-learning-based post-training. To reduce long-horizon rollout drift, we further introduce Sliding Window Re-encoding (SWR), a training-free inference strategy that periodically refreshes the generation context. Under our in-domain evaluation protocol, RoboAlign-R1 improves the aggregate six-dimension score by 10.1% over the strongest baseline, including gains of 7.5% on Manipulation Accuracy and 4.6% on Instruction Following; these ranking improvements are further supported by an external VLM-based cross-check and a blinded human study. Meanwhile, SWR improves long-horizon prediction quality with only about 1% additional latency, yielding a 2.8% gain in SSIM and a 9.8% reduction in LPIPS. Together, these results show that reward-aligned post-training and stabilized long-horizon decoding improve task consistency, physical realism, and long-horizon prediction quality in robot video world models.

</details>


### [106] [SigLoMa: Learning Open-World Quadrupedal Loco-Manipulation from Ego-Centric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03846)
*Shiyi Chen,Haiyi Liu,Mingye Yang,Jiaqi Zhang,Debing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SigLoMa是一种完全机载的、基于自我中心视觉的四足机器人操作框架，通过引入轻量级几何表示Sigma Points和自我中心卡尔曼滤波器，实现了在低频感知下高效、鲁棒的动态移动操作，无需依赖外部设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法在开放环境中存在样本效率低、仿真到现实差距大以及视觉延迟与高频率控制需求冲突的问题，且依赖昂贵的外部动捕系统。因此，需要一种完全自主、高效的四足操作框架。

Method: 提出Sigma Points作为轻量级外部感知几何表示，设计自我中心卡尔曼滤波器以融合低频感知与高频控制，采用基于Hint Poses的主动采样课程学习提升样本效率，并结合时间编码与模拟随机游走漂移解决视觉盲区问题。

Result: 在仅依赖5Hz（200ms延迟）开放词汇检测器的情况下，SigLoMa在真实世界中成功实现了多种动态移动操作任务，性能接近专家级人工遥操作水平。

Conclusion: SigLoMa实现了无需外部依赖的高性能四足机器人动态操作，展示了在低频感知条件下实现高效、鲁棒控制的可行性，推动了开放环境中自主机器人系统的发展。

Abstract: Designing an open-world quadrupedal loco-manipulation system is highly challenging. Traditional reinforcement learning frameworks utilizing exteroception often suffer from extreme sample inefficiency and massive sim-to-real gaps. Furthermore, the inherent latency of visual tracking fundamentally conflicts with the high-frequency demands of precise floating-base control. Consequently, existing systems lean heavily on expensive external motion capture and off-board computation. To eliminate these dependencies, we present SigLoMa, a fully onboard, ego-centric vision-based pick-and-place framework. At the core of SigLoMa is the introduction of Sigma Points, a lightweight geometric representation for exteroception that guarantees high scalability and native sim-to-real alignment. To bridge the frequency divide between slow perception and fast control, we design an ego-centric Kalman Filter to provide robust, high-rate state estimation. On the learning front, we alleviate sample inefficiency via an Active Sampling Curriculum guided by Hint Poses, and tackle the robot's structural visual blind spots using temporal encoding coupled with simulated random-walk drift. Real-world experiments validate that, relying solely on a 5Hz (200 ms latency) open-vocabulary detector, SigLoMa successfully executes dynamic loco-manipulation across multiple tasks, achieving performance comparable to expert human teleoperation.

</details>


### [107] [Task-Aware Scanning Parameter Configuration for Robotic Inspection Using Vision Language Embeddings and Hyperdimensional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03909)
*Zhiling Chen,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Yang Zhang,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于任务指令和场景观测的机器人激光扫描传感器参数自动推荐方法，通过构建多模态数据集Instruct-Obs2Param和超维度计算框架ScanHD，实现了高精度、低延迟的自适应传感配置，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业激光轮廓仪的测量性能常受限于复杂的传感器参数调优，现有方法依赖人工试错，效率低且难以恢复因参数不匹配导致的测量失败，因此需要一种能根据任务意图和环境上下文自动推荐最优参数的方法。

Method: 提出ScanHD框架，利用超维度计算将自然语言指令和RGB预扫描图像编码为任务感知码，并通过紧凑内存进行参数级关联推理；同时构建真实世界多模态数据集Instruct-Obs2Param用于评测。

Result: 在Instruct-Obs2Param数据集上，ScanHD在五个关键参数上达到92.7%的平均精确准确率和98.1%的平均Win@1准确率，具有良好的跨分割泛化能力和低延迟推理性能，优于基于规则的方法、传统多模态模型和多模态大语言模型。

Conclusion: 本研究实现了从任务意图和场景上下文出发的自主、指令驱动的传感配置，将传感器参数设置由静态配置转变为可适应的决策变量，消除了人工调参需求。

Abstract: Robotic laser profiling is widely used for dimensional verification and surface inspection, yet measurement fidelity is often dominated by sensor configuration rather than robot motion. Industrial profilers expose multiple coupled parameters, including sampling frequency, measurement range, exposure time, receiver dynamic range, and illumination, that are still tuned by trial-and-error; mismatches can cause saturation, clipping, or missing returns that cannot be recovered downstream. We formulate instruction-conditioned sensing parameter recommendation; given a pre-scan RGB observation and a natural-language inspection instruction, infer a discrete configuration over key parameters of a robot-mounted profiler. To benchmark this problem, we develop Instruct-Obs2Param, a real-world multimodal dataset linking inspection intents and multi-view pose and illumination variation across 16 objects to canonical parameter regimes. We then propose ScanHD, a hyperdimensional computing framework that binds instruction and observation into a task-aware code and performs parameter-wise associative reasoning with compact memories, matching discrete scanner regimes while yielding stable, interpretable, low-latency decisions. On Instruct-Obs2Param, ScanHD achieves 92.7% average exact accuracy and 98.1% average Win@1 accuracy across the five parameters, with strong cross-split generalization and low-latency inference suitable for deployment, outperforming rule-based heuristics, conventional multimodal models, and multimodal large language models. This work enables autonomous, instruction-conditioned sensing configuration from task intent and scene context, eliminating manual tuning and elevating sensor configuration from a static setting to an adaptive decision variable.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [CreativityBench: Evaluating Agent Creative Reasoning via Affordance-Based Tool Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02910)
*Cheng Qian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Jiayu Liu,Bingxiang He,Jeonghwan Kim,Jiateng Liu,Bingxuan Li,Aditi Tiwari,Dwip Dalal,Zhenhailong Wang,Xiusi Chen,Mahdi Namazifar,Yunzhu Li,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CreativityBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在基于功能的创造性工具使用方面能力的新基准。通过构建包含4K个实体和15万条以上功能标注的知识库，生成了1.4万个需要非显而易见但物理上可行解法的任务。对10个最先进大模型的评估表明，当前模型在识别正确部件、功能及物理机制方面仍存在显著不足，且模型规模扩大和推理策略改进带来的提升有限，说明创造性工具使用仍是当前模型的重大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在创造性问题解决方面的能力，尤其是基于对象功能和属性进行工具重用的能力，弥补现有研究多关注推理与环境交互任务而忽视创造力评估的空白。

Method: 构建大规模的功能知识库（KB），涵盖对象、部件、属性和可操作用途之间的显式关联；基于该知识库生成具有约束条件的非显而易见任务集CreativityBench；在10个主流大语言模型上进行系统评估，分析其在对象选择、部件识别、功能推理和机制理解等方面的表现。

Result: 实验显示，尽管部分模型能选出合理对象，但在识别关键部件、相关功能和物理机制方面表现不佳，导致整体性能大幅下降；模型规模提升带来的改进迅速饱和；强大的通用推理能力无法有效迁移到创造性功能发现任务；链式思维等常见推理策略增益有限。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在创造性工具使用方面仍存在明显缺陷，CreativityBench为评估这一智能维度提供了有效测试平台，对未来发展具备规划与推理能力的智能体具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have led to strong performance on reasoning and environment-interaction tasks, yet their ability for creative problem-solving remains underexplored. We study this capability through the lens of creative tool use, where a model repurposes available objects by reasoning about their affordances and attributes rather than relying on canonical usage. As a first step, we introduce CreativityBench, a benchmark for evaluating affordance-based creativity in LLMs. To this end, we build a large-scale affordance knowledge base (KB) with 4K entities and 150K+ affordance annotations, explicitly linking objects, parts, attributes, and actionable uses. Building on this KB, we generate 14K grounded tasks that require identifying non-obvious yet physically plausible solutions under constraints. Evaluations across 10 state-of-the-art LLMs, including closed and open-source models, show that models can often select a plausible object, but fail to identify the correct parts, their affordances, and the underlying physical mechanism needed to solve the task, leading to a significant drop in performance. Furthermore, improvements from model scaling quickly saturate, strong general reasoning does not reliably translate to creative affordance discovery, and common inference-time strategies such as Chain-of-Thought yield limited gains. These results suggest that creative tool use remains a major challenge for current models, and that CreativityBench provides a useful testbed for studying this missing dimension of intelligence, with potential implications for planning and reasoning modules in future agents.

</details>


### [109] [Computing Thiele Rules on Interval Elections and their Generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03067)
*Dimitris Avramidis,Alexandra Lassota,Ulrike Schmidt-Kraepelin,Adrian Vetta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于批准的委员会投票中的Thiele规则在不同偏好结构下的计算复杂性，证明了在选民区间（VI）域上尽管约束矩阵非全单模，标准线性规划仍存在最优整数解，并提出了快速求解算法；同时扩展到更广义的VCI和LC域，揭示了二者关系，并指出树状推广下问题变为NP难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thiele规则（如PAV）具有良好性质但通常计算困难（NP-hard），在特定结构化偏好下可能高效计算，然而其在选民区间（VI）域上的复杂性长期未决，亟需解决。

Method: 通过分析线性规划的约束矩阵性质，结合图论方法研究解的整性；引入新的定义方式揭示LC与VCI域的关系，并构造树状推广以探讨计算复杂性的边界。

Result: 1. 在VI域上，尽管矩阵非全单模，标准LP仍存在最优整数解，并提出快速算法；2. LC域严格包含VCI域；3. 给出LC域的新等价定义，具有选举解释意义；4. 树状推广的VCI域上Thiele规则计算为NP难。

Conclusion: Thiele规则在VI、VCI及LC等结构化偏好域上仍可在多项式时间内计算，但在更复杂的树状结构上变得难解，揭示了结构限制对计算复杂性的影响边界。

Abstract: Approval-based committee voting has received significant attention in the social choice community. Among the studied rules, Thiele rules, and especially Proportional Approval Voting (PAV), stand out for desirable properties such as proportional representation, Pareto optimality, and support monotonicity. Their main drawback is that computing a Thiele outcome is NP-hard in general.
  A glimpse of hope comes from the fact that Thiele rules are better behaved under structured preferences. On the candidate interval (CI) domain, they are computable in polynomial time via a linear program (LP) that has a totally unimodular constraint matrix. Surprisingly, this approach fails for the related voter interval (VI) domain, and the complexity of the problem has repeatedly been posed as an open question. Our main result resolves this question: although the relevant matrix is not totally unimodular, the ``standard'' LP still admits at least one optimal integral solution, and we provide a fast algorithm for finding it.
  Our technique naturally extends to the voter-candidate interval (VCI) domain, also known as the 1-dimensional voter-candidate range (1D-VCR) domain, and to the linearly consistent (LC) domain, both of which generalize the candidate and voter interval domains. Although both the VCI and LC domains have been studied in social choice, their relationship was unknown. We show, through connections to graph theory, that LC strictly contains VCI. We also provide an alternative definition of LC that is closer in spirit to VCI and has a natural interpretation in approval elections; this equivalence may be of independent interest. Finally, we study an alternative tree-based generalization of VCI and show that Thiele rules become NP-hard to compute on this domain.

</details>


### [110] [Making the Invisible Visible: Understanding the Mismatch Between Organizational Goals and Worker Experiences in AI Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03078)
*Christine P. Lee,Min Kyung Lee,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI在组织中应用时因员工抵制和整合困难而导致的失败问题，强调应将员工视为AI整合的核心，并提出多层次适应策略以促进AI与实际工作实践的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示AI系统在组织中实施失败的原因，特别是关注员工在AI设计与使用决策中的边缘化问题，以及组织期望与员工实际体验之间的脱节。

Method: 通过访谈医疗、金融和管理领域日常使用AI系统的专业人士，识别AI整合过程中的关键障碍。

Result: 发现了影响AI有效整合的主要障碍，包括可用性和互操作性差、期望不一致、控制权有限以及沟通不足。

Conclusion: 成功的AI采用需要将员工置于核心位置，并在个人、任务和组织层面采取适应性策略，以更好地使AI系统与真实工作流程相匹配。

Abstract: While AI is often introduced into organizations to drive innovation and efficiency, many adoption efforts fail as workers resist and struggle to integrate these systems. These failures point to a deeper issue: workers, the very people expected to collaborate with AI, are often invisible in decisions about how AI is designed and used. Drawing on interviews with professionals who interact with AI systems daily in healthcare, finance, and management, we examine the disconnect between organizational expectations and worker experiences. We identify key barriers, including poor usability and interoperability, misaligned expectations, limited control, and insufficient communication. These challenges highlight a gap between how organizations implement AI and the evolving worker needs, tasks, and workflows that it fails to support. We argue that successful adoption requires recognizing workers as central to AI integration and propose adaptation strategies at the individual, task, and organizational levels to better align AI systems with real-world practices.

</details>


### [111] [Programmatic Context Augmentation for LLM-based Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03101)
*Hao Liu,Xiao-Wen Yang,Atharva Sehgal,Yixin Wang,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合程序化上下文增强的大型语言模型（LLM）进化搜索框架，用于符号回归任务，通过代码交互实现更丰富的数据信息提取，提升了效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统符号回归方法受限于可扩展性和表达能力，现有LLM方法仅依赖标量评估指标，忽略了数据中的丰富信息。

Method: 引入程序化上下文增强的LLM-based进化搜索框架，允许通过代码与数据集进行交互，主动执行数据分析以提取超越聚合评分的信息信号。

Result: 在LLM-SRBench等先进基准上验证，相比强基线方法展现出更高的效率和准确性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用数据内在信息，提升了符号回归的性能，为科学发现中的数学表达式发现提供了新思路。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR), the task of discovering mathematical expressions that best describe a given dataset, remains a fundamental challenge in scientific discovery. Traditional approaches, primarily based on genetic algorithms and related evolutionary methods, have proven useful but suffer from scalability and expressivity limitations. Recently, large language model (LLM)-based evolutionary search methods have been introduced into SR and show promise. However, existing LLM-based approaches typically rely on scalar evaluation metrics, such as mean squared error, as the sole source of feedback during the search process, thereby overlooking the rich information embedded in the dataset. To address this limitation, we propose a novel LLM-based evolutionary search framework that incorporates programmatic context augmentation. By enabling code-based interactions with the dataset, our method can actively perform data analysis and extract informative signals, beyond aggregated evaluation scores. We evaluate our framework on advanced benchmarks, such as LLM-SRBench, and demonstrate superior efficiency and accuracy compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Stop Automating Peer Review Without Rigorous Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03202)
*Joachim Baumann,Jiaxin Pei,Sanmi Koyejo,Dirk Hovy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了当前AI系统在生成学术论文评审中的局限性，指出AI评审存在过度一致性和易被风格化改写欺骗的问题，强调实现自动化评审需要专门的科学研究而非直接使用通用大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对同行评审危机，探索大型语言模型是否可用于自动生成论文评审，并揭示现有AI系统的不足之处。

Method: 通过比较人类与AI生成的ICLR 2026论文评审，以及评估自动改写论文对不同AI评审者的影响，进行实证分析。

Result: 发现AI评审存在群体思维效应，评审意见过于一致，缺乏多样性；且通过提示LLM重写论文即可显著提高AI给出的评分，显示其容易被游戏化。

Conclusion: 非可游戏性和评审多样性是自动化评审的必要但不充分条件，解决同行评审危机需建立评审自动化科学，而非依赖未经严格评估的通用大模型。

Abstract: Large language models offer a tempting solution to address the peer review crisis. This position paper argues that today's AI systems should not be used to produce paper reviews. We ground this position in an empirical comparison of human- versus AI-generated ICLR 2026 reviews and an evaluation of the effect of automated paper rewriting on different AI reviewers. We identify two critical issues: 1) AI reviewers exhibit a hivemind effect of excessive agreement within and across papers that reduces perspective diversity. 2) AI review scores are trivially gameable through paper laundering: prompting an LLM to rewrite a paper could significantly increase the scores from AI reviewers, demonstrating that LLM reviewers are easy to game through stylistic changes rather than scientific results. However, non-gameability and review diversity are necessary but not sufficient conditions for automation. We argue that addressing the peer review crisis requires a science of peer review automation -- not general-purpose LLMs deployed without rigorous evaluation.

</details>


### [113] [Evaluating Prompting and Execution-Based Methods for Deterministic Computation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03227)
*Hongkun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了多种提示策略在精确计算任务上的表现，发现程序化思维（PoT）和专用小模型能实现完美准确率，表明大语言模型需结合外部工具或专用模型以可靠执行确定性任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在需要精确、无错误输出的任务中进行确定性计算的能力是否可靠。

Method: 评估链式思维（CoT）、由简至繁分解、程序化思维（PoT）和自一致性（SC）等提示策略，在二进制计数、最长子串检测和算术求值等任务上的表现，并构建合成数据集进行受控评估，同时训练小型领域特定模型CodeT5-small生成可执行程序。

Result: 标准提示方法在序列任务上准确率有限；CoT改进有限，由简至繁存在误差累积；PoT通过生成可执行代码并交由外部解释器实现完全准确；自一致性通过多数投票提升鲁棒性但开销大；训练的小型模型CodeT5-small在所有任务上达到完美准确率且训练成本低。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可能仅模拟推理过程，无法可靠执行精确符号计算；对于确定性任务，结合外部工具或使用专用模型是更可靠高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and reasoning. However, their ability to perform exact, deterministic computation remains unclear. In this work, we systematically evaluate multiple prompting strategies, including Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Least-to-Most decomposition, Program-of-Thought (PoT), and Self-Consistency (SC), on tasks requiring precise and error-free outputs, including binary counting, longest substring detection, and arithmetic evaluation.
  To support this study, we introduce a synthetic dataset with diverse natural language instructions, enabling controlled evaluation of exact computation across multiple task types.
  Our results show that standard prompting methods achieve only moderate accuracy on sequence-based tasks. CoT provides limited improvement, while Least-to-Most suffers from error accumulation. In contrast, PoT achieves perfect accuracy by generating executable code and delegating computation to an external interpreter. Self-Consistency improves robustness through majority voting, but incurs substantial computational overhead.
  We further train a small domain-specific model (CodeT5-small) to generate executable programs, which achieves perfect accuracy on held-out synthetic test data across all tasks with minimal training cost.
  Overall, our findings suggest that LLMs may simulate reasoning patterns rather than reliably perform exact symbolic computation. For deterministic tasks, combining LLMs with external tools or using specialized models provides a more reliable and efficient solution.

</details>


### [114] [Revisiting the Travel Planning Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03308)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Jin Ye,Peng-Yu Hua,Jia-Wei Cao,Jie-Jing Shao,Yu-Feng Li,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的解耦评估方法，将旅行规划分解为五个原子子能力，以更精确地评估大语言模型（LLM）在各环节的表现，揭示了其在隐式约束推断、计划生成和自我纠错方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旅行规划基准主要进行端到端的最终计划评估，缺乏可解释性，难以定位失败的根本原因。因此需要一种更细粒度的评估方式来识别LLM在具体推理步骤中的缺陷。

Method: 将旅行规划任务分解为五个原子子能力：约束提取、工具使用、计划生成、错误识别和错误纠正，并采用基于oracle中间上下文的解耦评估协议，独立测量每个组件的性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLMs在提取显式约束方面表现良好，但在推断隐式开放世界需求时存在困难；在计划生成中表现出结构性偏差，在自我纠错时则过于敏感且容易持续错误。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度评估揭示了LLMs在复杂推理任务中的具体弱点，为改进其推理与规划能力提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Travel planning serves as a critical task for long-horizon reasoning, exposing significant deficits in LLMs. However, existing benchmarks and evaluations primarily assess final plans in an end-to-end manner, which lacks interpretability and makes it difficult to analyze the root causes of failures. To bridge this gap, we decompose travel planning into five constituent atomic sub-capabilities, including \emph{Constraint Extraction}, \emph{Tool Use}, \emph{Plan Generation}, \emph{Error Identification}, and \emph{Error Correction}. We implement a decoupled evaluation protocol leveraging oracle intermediate contexts to rigorously isolate these components, thereby measuring the atomic performance boundary without the noise of cascading errors. Our results highlight a clear contrast in performance: while LLMs are proficient in extracting explicit constraints, they struggle to infer implicit, open-world requirements. Furthermore, they exhibit structural biases in plan generation and suffer from ineffective self-correction, characterized by excessive sensitivity and erroneous persistence. These findings offer precise directions for improving LLM reasoning and planning abilities.

</details>


### [115] [Automated Large-scale CVRP Solver Design via LLM-assisted Flexible MCTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03339)
*Tong Guo,Caishun Chen,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大语言模型的灵活蒙特卡洛树搜索框架（LaF-MCTS），用于自动化设计高性能的大规模车辆路径问题（LSCVRP）求解器，通过三层决策结构和语义剪枝等机制，在CVRPLib上实现了优于现有最先进求解器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模车辆路径问题（LSCVRP）难以被现有求解器有效处理，且传统的分治法依赖专家经验进行分解策略设计，成本高、扩展性差，亟需一种自动化的高效算法设计方法。

Method: 提出LaF-MCTS框架，构建三层决策层次以逐步生成分解策略与子求解器；引入语义剪枝去除冗余代码，并通过分支再生保持搜索多样性，利用大语言模型在受限上下文中高效探索算法假设空间。

Result: 在CVRPLib数据集上的实验表明，LaF-MCTS能够自主组合并优化出超越多种最先进求解器的LSCVRP求解方案，显著提升求解效率与质量。

Conclusion: LaF-MCTS为利用大语言模型自动化设计复杂组合优化算法提供了有效范式，尤其适用于大规模车辆路径问题的求解，具有较强的可扩展性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Solving large-scale CVRP (LSCVRP) with hundreds to thousands of nodes remains difficult for even state-of-the-art solvers. Divide-and-conquer can scale by decomposing the instance into size-reduced subproblems, but designing decomposition logic and configuring sub-solvers is highly expertise- and labor-intensive. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as promising tools for automated algorithm design. However, existing LLM-driven approaches struggle with LSCVRP primarily due to the difficulty in generating sophisticated search strategies within a limited context window. To bridge this gap, we propose the LLM-assisted Flexible Monte Carlo Tree Search (LaF-MCTS), a novel framework that automates the design of high-performance LSCVRP solvers. We develop a three-tier decision hierarchy to enable incremental design of decomposition policies and sub-solvers for LSCVRP. To enable efficient search within the algorithmic hypothesis space, we introduce semantic pruning to eliminate semantically and structurally redundant codes, and branch regrowth to regenerate codes and preserve diversity. Extensive experiments on CVRPLib demonstrate that LaF-MCTS autonomously composes and optimizes decomposition-enhanced solvers that surpasses various state-of-the-art CVRP solvers.

</details>


### [116] [Robust Agent Compensation (RAC): Teaching AI Agents to Compensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03409)
*Srinath Perera,Kaviru Hapuarachchi,Frank Leymann,Rania Khalaf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Robust Agent Compensation (RAC)的日志型恢复机制，可集成到大多数Agent框架中以提升执行可靠性，且无需修改现有代码。基于LangChain的实现表明，RAC在复杂任务中的延迟和令牌消耗方面比现有LLM恢复方法高效1.5-8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决Agent在执行复杂任务时可能出现的不可靠行为和意外副作用，缺乏高效、通用的恢复机制的问题。

Method: 提出RAC，一种基于日志的恢复范式，通过架构扩展实现，并利用现有Agent框架的扩展点进行集成，无需更改用户原有代码。

Result: 在τ-bench和REALM-Bench上验证了RAC的有效性，实验显示其在解决复杂问题时，相比当前最先进的基于LLM的恢复方法，在延迟和令牌经济性方面提升了1.5至8倍以上。

Conclusion: RAC是一种高效、通用且易于集成的恢复机制，能显著提升Agent系统的可靠性和资源效率，适用于多种现有Agent框架。

Abstract: We present Robust Agent Compensation (RAC), a log-based recovery paradigm (providing a safety net) implemented through an architectural extension that can be applied to most Agent frameworks to support reliable executions (avoiding unintended side effects). Users can choose to enable RAC without changing their current agent code (e.g., LangGraph agents). The proposed approach can be implemented in most existing agent frameworks via their existing extension points. We present an implementation based on LangChain, demonstrate its viability through the $τ$-bench and REALM-Bench, and show that when solving complex problems, RAC is 1.5-8X or more better in both latency and token economy compared to state-of-the-art LLM-based recovery approaches.

</details>


### [117] [Geometry over Density: Few-Shot Cross-Domain OOD Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03410)
*Shawn Li,You Qin,Jiate Li,Charith Peris,Lisa Bauer,Roger Zimmermann,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为UFCOD的统一框架，用于实现一次性训练、任意部署的跨域OOD检测，仅需少量ID样本即可在不同领域间高效检测异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的OOD检测方法通常针对单一训练分布进行设计，难以适应新任务且需要大量标注数据；本文旨在解决如何利用预训练模型在仅有少量ID样本的情况下，实现对任意新ID-OOD任务对的高效跨域异常检测问题。

Method: 基于扩散模型中的噪声预测作为得分函数（log-density梯度），提出两种能量特征：路径能量（积分得分幅度）和动态能量（得分平滑性），构成离散Sobolev范数，并通过信息几何分析扩散轨迹来实现无需再训练的OOD检测。

Result: 在一个数据集上预训练的扩散模型可作为通用特征提取器应用于语义无关的多个新领域（如CelebA→CIFAR-10/SVHN/Textures等），每任务仅需约100个无标签ID样本，平均AUROC达到93.7%，性能媲美使用5万至16万样本训练的方法，样本效率提升约500倍。

Conclusion: UFCOD实现了“一次训练、处处部署”的跨域OOD检测范式，在极低样本需求下保持优异性能，显著提升了模型泛化能力与部署灵活性。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection identifies test samples that fall outside a model's training distribution, a capability critical for safe deployment in high-stakes applications. Standard OOD detectors are trained on a specific in-distribution (ID) dataset and detect deviations from that single domain. In contrast, we study few-shot cross-domain OOD detection: given a \emph{single} pre-trained model, can we perform OOD detection on \emph{arbitrary} new ID-OOD task pairs using only a handful of ID samples at inference time, with no additional training? We propose \textbf{UFCOD}, a unified framework that achieves this goal through information-geometric analysis of diffusion trajectories. Our key insight is that diffusion noise predictions are score functions (gradients of log-density), and we extract two energy features: \emph{Path Energy} (integrated score magnitude) and \emph{Dynamics Energy} (score smoothness), that form a discrete Sobolev norm capturing how samples interact with the learned diffusion process. The central contribution is a \textbf{train-once, deploy-anywhere} paradigm: a diffusion model trained on a single dataset (e.g., CelebA) serves as a universal feature extractor for OOD detection across semantically unrelated domains (e.g., CIFAR-10, SVHN, Textures). At deployment, each new task requires only $\sim$100 unlabeled ID samples for inference: no retraining, no fine-tuning, no task-specific adaptation. Using 100 ID samples per task, UFCOD achieves 93.7\% average AUROC across 12 cross-domain benchmarks, competitive with methods trained on 50k--163k samples, demonstrating $\sim$500$\times$ improvement in sample efficiency. See our code in https://github.com/lili0415/UFCOD.

</details>


### [118] [Steerable Adversarial Scenario Generation through Test-Time Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20102)
*Tong Nie,Yuewen Mei,Yihong Tang,Junlin He,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可调控的对抗性场景生成框架SAGE，通过多目标偏好对齐和权重插值实现测试时对抗性与真实性的精细平衡，无需重训练即可灵活生成多样化驾驶测试场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性场景生成方法在对抗性和真实性之间缺乏灵活权衡，难以满足多样化的训练和测试需求，限制了自动驾驶系统的安全评估效率。

Method: 将对抗性场景生成重构为多目标偏好对齐问题，提出分层组偏好优化方法，并通过微调两个对立偏好的专家模型，在推理时线性插值其权重以构建连续策略谱。

Result: 实验表明SAGE在对抗性与真实性的平衡上优于现有方法，并能更有效地用于闭环驾驶策略训练。理论分析基于线性模式连通性支持该框架的合理性。

Conclusion: SAGE实现了无需重新训练的细粒度可控对抗场景生成，提升了自动驾驶安全评估的灵活性和效率。

Abstract: Adversarial scenario generation is a cost-effective approach for safety assessment of autonomous driving systems. However, existing methods are often constrained to a single, fixed trade-off between competing objectives such as adversariality and realism. This yields behavior-specific models that cannot be steered at inference time, lacking the efficiency and flexibility to generate tailored scenarios for diverse training and testing requirements. In view of this, we reframe the task of adversarial scenario generation as a multi-objective preference alignment problem and introduce a new framework named \textbf{S}teerable \textbf{A}dversarial scenario \textbf{GE}nerator (SAGE). SAGE enables fine-grained test-time control over the trade-off between adversariality and realism without any retraining. We first propose hierarchical group-based preference optimization, a data-efficient offline alignment method that learns to balance competing objectives by decoupling hard feasibility constraints from soft preferences. Instead of training a fixed model, SAGE fine-tunes two experts on opposing preferences and constructs a continuous spectrum of policies at inference time by linearly interpolating their weights. We provide theoretical justification for this framework through the lens of linear mode connectivity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SAGE not only generates scenarios with a superior balance of adversariality and realism but also enables more effective closed-loop training of driving policies. Project page: https://tongnie.github.io/SAGE/.

</details>


### [119] [Adaptive Dual-Path Framework for Covert Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03423)
*Xi Yu,Weicai Li,Lin Yin,Tiejun Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的自适应双路径框架，用于任务导向的隐蔽语义通信，通过语义级内嵌编码在任务特征中嵌入隐蔽信息，在保证主要语义任务性能的同时实现了接近随机猜测的高安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统隐蔽通信方法通常依赖功率域信号叠加嵌入隐秘信息，难以兼顾通信效率与安全性。本文旨在通过语义通信框架，将隐秘传输与任务需求结合，提升隐蔽性与实用性。

Method: 提出一种自适应双路径框架，包含显式路径（公共任务）和隐写路径（联合编码公共与隐蔽信息），通过对比表示对齐实现语义级嵌入，并采用Gumbel-Softmax机制进行自适应模块选择，构建多目标优化问题以同时优化语义理解与隐蔽传输。

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集上实验表明，该方法将攻击者检测准确率压制至56.12%，接近随机猜测水平，同时在主语义任务上的性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效实现了高隐蔽性与高效语义通信的统一，为安全语义通信提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel adaptive dual-path framework for covert semantic communication (SemCom), which integrates covert information transmission with task-oriented semantic coding. Unlike conventional covert communication methods that embed hidden messages through power-domain signal superposition, our framework embeds covert data within task-specific features via semantic-level intrinsic encoding. This new architecture introduces dual encoding paths with adaptive block selection: an Explicit path for public task execution and a Stego path that jointly encodes both public and covert information through contrastive representation alignment. A Gumbel-Softmax enabled adaptive path selection mechanism dynamically activates network blocks based on task require- ments. We formulate a multi-objective optimization framework that simultaneously ensures accurate semantic understanding and reliable covert transmission. We rigorously evaluate our framework's security against a powerful, independently trained attacker. Experimental results on the Cityscapes dataset demon- strate a state-of-the-art level of covertness: our method suppresses the attacker's detection accuracy to a near-random guessing level of 56.12%. This robust security is achieved while simultaneously maintaining superior performance on the primary semantic tasks compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [120] [FinSTaR: Towards Financial Reasoning with Time Series Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03460)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对金融时间序列推理的2x2能力分类法，并构建了FinTSR-Bench基准和FinSTaR模型，通过定制化的思维链策略在确定性评估与随机性预测任务中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列推理模型在金融领域表现不佳，因金融数据具有独特特性，需专门建模其确定性评估与随机性预测能力。

Method: 提出2x2能力分类法（单实体/多实体 × 当前状态评估/未来行为预测），构建包含十个金融推理任务的FinTSR-Bench基准；设计FinSTaR模型，分别采用Compute-in-CoT（程序化思维链）处理确定性评估任务，Scenario-Aware CoT（情景感知思维链）应对随机性预测任务。

Result: FinSTaR在FinTSR-Bench上平均准确率达78.9%，显著优于现有大模型和时间序列推理模型；验证了四类能力可通过联合训练相互增强，且Scenario-Aware CoT持续提升预测性能。

Conclusion: 针对金融时间序列的复杂性，需区分确定性与随机性任务并采用相应推理机制，所提分类法、基准与模型为金融领域的时间序列推理提供了有效框架与新方向。

Abstract: Time series (TS) reasoning models (TSRMs) have shown promising capabilities in general domains, yet they consistently fail on financial domain, which exhibit unique characteristics. We propose a general 2x2 capability taxonomy for TSRMs by crossing 1) single-entity vs. multi-entity analysis with 2) assessment of the current state vs. prediction of future behavior. We instantiate this taxonomy in the financial domain -- where the distinction between deterministic assessment and stochastic prediction is particularly critical -- as ten financial reasoning tasks, forming the FinTSR-Bench benchmark based on S&P stocks. To this end, we propose FinSTaR (Financial Time Series Thinking and Reasoning), trained on FinTSR-Bench with distinct chain-of-thought (CoT) strategies tailored to each category. For assessment, which is deterministic (i.e., computable from observable data), we employ Compute-in-CoT, a programmatic CoT that enables models to derive answers directly from raw prices. For prediction, which is inherently stochastic (i.e., subject to unobservable factors), we adopt Scenario-Aware CoT, which generates diverse scenarios before making a judgment, mirroring how financial analysts reason under uncertainty. The proposed method achieves 78.9% average accuracy on FinTSR-Bench, substantially outperforming LLM and TSRM baselines. Furthermore, we show that the four capability categories are complementary and mutually reinforcing through joint training, and that Scenario-Aware CoT consistently improves prediction accuracy over standard CoT. Code is publicly available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/FinSTaR.

</details>


### [121] [SOAR: Real-Time Joint Optimization of Order Allocation and Robot Scheduling in Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03842)
*Yibang Tang,Yifan Yang,Jingyuan Wang,Junhua Chen,Zhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习的统一框架SOAR，用于机器人移动履行系统中的实时联合优化，有效提升了仓储效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在保证响应速度时牺牲了全局最优性，或因计算成本过高而不适用于动态工业环境，因此需要一种兼顾实时性与全局优化性能的方法。

Method: 提出SOAR框架，将订单分配和机器人调度统一建模为事件驱动的马尔可夫决策过程，采用异构图变换器编码仓库状态，并引入奖励塑形策略以应对长周期任务中的稀疏反馈问题。

Result: 在合成和真实工业数据集上的实验表明，SOAR将全局完工时间减少7.5%，平均订单完成时间降低15.4%，且推理延迟低于100毫秒；仿真到现实的部署验证了其在实际生产环境中的有效性。

Conclusion: SOAR实现了高效、低延迟的实时联合优化，具有良好的实用性和显著的性能提升，适用于复杂的动态仓储场景。

Abstract: Robotic Mobile Fulfillment Systems (RMFS) rely on mobile robots for automated inventory transportation, coordinating order allocation and robot scheduling to enhance warehousing efficiency. However, optimizing RMFS is challenging due to strict real-time constraints and the strong coupling of multi-phase decisions. Existing methods either decompose the problem into isolated sub-tasks to guarantee responsiveness at the cost of global optimality, or rely on computationally expensive global optimization models that are unsuitable for dynamic industrial environments. To bridge this gap, we propose SOAR, a unified Deep Reinforcement Learning framework for real-time joint optimization. SOAR transforms order allocation and robot scheduling into a unified process by utilizing soft order allocations as observations. We formulate this as an Event-Driven Markov Decision Process, enabling the agent to perform simultaneous scheduling in response to asynchronous system events. Technically, we employ a Heterogeneous Graph Transformer to encode the warehouse state and integrate phased domain knowledge. Additionally, we incorporate a reward shaping strategy to address sparse feedback in long-horizon tasks. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world industrial datasets, in collaboration with Geekplus, demonstrate that SOAR reduces global makespan by 7.5\% and average order completion time by 15.4\% with sub-100ms latency. Furthermore, sim-to-real deployment confirms its practical viability and significant performance gains in production environments. The code is available at https://github.com/200815147/SOAR.

</details>


### [122] [Real-Time Evaluation of Autonomous Systems under Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03491)
*Adithya Mohan,Xujun Xie,Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于真实世界交叉路口驾驶数据的离线轨迹学习与对抗鲁棒性评估框架，比较了三种轨迹学习范式，并揭示了模型结构设计对对抗稳定性的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯仿真环境下的自动驾驶策略评估无法反映真实世界中的结构不一致性和状态表示效应，因此需要基于真实数据的评估框架来更准确地衡量策略的鲁棒性。

Method: 使用真实世界的交叉路口驾驶数据，在受控数据协议下训练并比较三种轨迹学习方法：基于MLP的行为克隆、基于Transformer的对象标记化行为克隆，以及基于GAIL框架的逆强化学习；通过ADE和FDE指标进行评估，并在推理时施加基于梯度的对抗扰动以构建鲁棒性评估矩阵。

Result: 三种模型在名义预测精度上相近（ADE < 0.08），但对抗稳定性差异显著；推理时的PGD攻击可导致最高约8米的最终位移误差，表明状态结构设计和模型归纳偏置对鲁棒性有关键影响。

Conclusion: 模型的状态结构设计和架构中的归纳偏置显著影响其对抗稳定性，仅依赖名义性能指标不足以评估自动驾驶策略的可靠性；所提框架为真实场景下的离线轨迹学习与鲁棒性评估提供了可扩展的基准。

Abstract: Most evaluations of autonomous driving policies under adversarial conditions are conducted in simulation, due to cost efficiency and the absence of physical risk. However, purely virtual testing fails to capture structural inconsistencies, supervision constraints, and state-representation effects that arise in real-world data and fundamentally shape policy robustness. This work presents an offline trajectory-learning and adversarial robustness evaluation framework grounded in real-world intersection driving data. Within a controlled data contract, we train and compare three trajectory-learning paradigms: Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP)-based Behavior Cloning (BC), Transformer-based object-tokenized BC, and inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) formulated within a Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (GAIL) framework. Models are evaluated using Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE).
  Inference-time robustness is assessed by subjecting trained policies to gradient-based adversarial perturbations across multiple intersection scenarios, yielding a structured robustness evaluation matrix. Results show that state-structure design and architectural inductive biases critically influence adversarial stability, leading to markedly different robustness profiles despite comparable nominal prediction accuracy (ADE < 0.08). Inference-time Projected Gradient Descent (PGD) attacks induce final displacement errors of up to approximately 8 meters. The proposed framework establishes a scalable benchmark for studying offline trajectory learning and adversarial robustness in real-world autonomous driving settings.

</details>


### [123] [Where Paths Split: Localized, Calibrated Control of Moral Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03609)
*Chenchen Yuan,Zheyu Zhang,Gjergji Kasneci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Convergent-Divergent Routing的方法，通过在Transformer块中定位并编辑道德相关路径的分支点，结合Dual Logit Calibration实现对大语言模型伦理框架的精确控制，同时保持其通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何在推理时引导大语言模型遵循特定的伦理框架，同时不损害其通用性能，解决模型在不同情境下道德偏好不一致的问题。

Method: 提出了Convergent-Divergent Routing方法，追踪并编辑Transformer模块中伦理路径首次汇聚再分离的关键分支点；采用Common Spatial Patterns从残差流中提取区分功利主义与道义论的方向，并通过Dual Logita Calibration进行闭式最小ℓ₂范数更新，以实现用户指定偏好的校准。

Result: 实验表明该方法能可靠地实现伦理偏好的校准，在真实道德困境任务上优于现有基线方法，且有效保持了模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够在不牺牲通用性能的前提下，精确调控大语言模型的伦理决策倾向，提供了一种可解释、细粒度的道德对齐机制。

Abstract: Large language models often display heterogeneous moral preferences across settings. We study inference-time steering toward a desired ethical framework while preserving general competence. We present Convergent-Divergent Routing, which traces and edits minimal branch points inside transformer blocks where ethical-framework-related pathways first converge and then diverge. Gating non-target branches at these loci blocks the downstream propagation while leaving upstream computations intact. We find that this intervention alone increases targeted ethical-framework reasoning. To achieve fine-grained control, we adapt Common Spatial Patterns to the residual stream and extract, for each branch-point layer, a pair of directions that discriminate between utilitarian and deontological frameworks. We then introduce Dual Logit Calibration, a closed-form, minimum-$\ell_2$-norm update that moves the residual within this two-dimensional subspace so the resulting directional projections align with user-specified preference weights. Experiments on real-life moral dilemmas show that our method reliably achieves preference calibration and largely preserves general capabilities, outperforming recent baselines while providing an interpretable mechanism.

</details>


### [124] [Self-Improvement for Fast, High-Quality Plan Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03625)
*Robert Gieselmann,Henrike von Huelsen,Mihai Samson,Marie-Christine Meyer,Dariusz Piotrowski,Oleksandr Radomskyi,Justin Okamoto,Turan Gojayev,Michael Painter,Gavin Brown,Federico Pecora,Jeremy L. Wyatt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成模型的自提升方法，用于在亚指数时间内生成高质量规划，实验表明该方法在多个领域中显著缩短了规划长度并提高了最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型多关注于找到可行解而非高质量解，而生成高质量规划是一个计算困难的问题，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用仅解码器的Transformer模型，结合自提升机制，通过多轮模型调用与图搜索生成更优规划，并用于模型微调；同时利用推理时搜索进一步提升规划质量。

Result: 在Blocksworld、Logistics、Labyrinth和Sokoban四个领域中，相比源符号规划器平均减少30%的规划长度，超过80%的规划为最优解，且模型延迟呈亚指数增长。

Conclusion: 基于生成模型的自提升方法为高质量规划生成提供了一种可扩展且高效的新途径。

Abstract: Generative models trained on synthetic plan data are a promising approach to generalized planning. Recent work has focused on finding any valid plan, rather than a high-quality solution. We address the challenge of producing high-quality plans, a computationally hard problem, in sub-exponential time. First, we demonstrate that, given optimal data, a decoder-only transformer can generate high-quality plans for unseen problem instances. Second, we show how to self-improve an initial model trained on sub-optimal data. Each round of self-improvement combines multiple model calls with graph search to generate improved plans, used for model fine-tuning. An experimental study on four domains: Blocksworld, Logistics, Labyrinth, and Sokoban, shows on average a 30% reduction in plan length over the source symbolic planner, with over 80% of plans being optimal, where the optimum is known. Plan quality is further improved by inference-time search. The model's latency scales sub-exponentially in contrast to the satisficing and optimal symbolic planners to which we compare. Together, these results suggest that self-improvement with generative models offers a scalable approach for high-quality plan generation.

</details>


### [125] [AdapShot: Adaptive Many-Shot In-Context Learning with Semantic-Aware KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03644)
*Jie Ou,Jinyu Guo,Shiyao Guo,Yuang Li,Ruiqi Wu,Zhaokun Wang,Wenyi Li,Wenhong Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AdapShot是一种动态优化示例数量并利用KV缓存重用的高效推理方法，通过基于探针的评估机制和语义感知的缓存策略，在多示例上下文学习中实现性能提升与速度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多-shot上下文学习方法采用固定示例数量，难以适应不同查询难度，且长上下文带来高昂计算与内存开销，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出AdapShot，设计基于输出熵的探针评估机制以动态确定最优示例数，并引入语义感知的KV缓存重用策略，结合解耦与重编码方法解决位置编码不兼容问题，实现高效推理。

Result: 实验表明，AdapShot相比当前最优的DBSA方法平均性能提升约10%，推理速度加快4.64倍。

Conclusion: AdapShot通过动态调整示例数量和高效的KV缓存重用，有效提升了多示例上下文学习的性能与效率，具有较强的实用价值。

Abstract: Many-Shot In-Context Learning (ICL) has emerged as a promising paradigm, leveraging extensive examples to unlock the reasoning potential of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, existing methods typically rely on a predetermined, fixed number of shots. This static approach often fails to adapt to the varying difficulty of different queries, leading to either insufficient context or interference from noise. Furthermore, the prohibitive computational and memory costs of long contexts severely limit Many-Shot's feasibility. To address the above limitations, we propose AdapShot, which dynamically optimizes shot counts and leverages KV cache reuse for efficient inference. Specifically, we design a probe-based evaluation mechanism that utilizes output entropy to determine the optimal number of shots. To bypass the redundant prefilling computation during both the probing and inference phases, we incorporate a semantics-aware KV cache reuse strategy. Within this reuse strategy, to address positional encoding incompatibilities, we introduce a decoupling and re-encoding method that enables the flexible reordering of cached key-value pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AdapShot achieves an average performance gain of around 10% and a 4.64x speedup compared to state-of-the-art DBSA.

</details>


### [126] [OracleProto: A Reproducible Framework for Benchmarking LLM Native Forecasting via Knowledge Cutoff and Temporal Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03762)
*Yiding Ma,Chengyun Ruan,Kaibo Huang,Zhongliang Yang,Linna Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OracleProto是一个可复现的框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）的原生预测能力，通过构建时间受限的预测样本并控制信息边界，有效区分真实预测与预训练知识泄露，显著降低数据泄露至1%水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM预测能力评估面临两难：实时基准无法复现，而回溯性基准难以避免模型已知知识的干扰。需要一个既能控制知识边界又可复现的评估框架。

Method: 提出OracleProto框架，结合模型截止日期对齐的样本筛选、工具级时间掩码、内容级泄露检测、离散答案归一化和分层评分，构建时间受限的预测样本，并在FutureX-Past衍生数据集上对六种主流LLM进行评估。

Result: OracleProto将数据泄露降至1%，比仅使用时间过滤的方法低一个数量级，能够有效区分不同模型的预测质量、采样稳定性和成本效率，支持可审计、可重用和可训练的预测能力评估。

Conclusion: OracleProto为LLM的预测能力提供了统一、公平且可控的评估接口，推动预测从一次性评测转变为可训练的数据级能力，适用于SFT和RL等下游应用。

Abstract: Large language models are moving from static text generators toward real-world decision-support systems, where forecasting is a composite capability that links information gathering, evidence integration, situational judgment, and action-oriented decision making. This capability is in broad demand across finance, policy, industry, and scientific research, yet its evaluation remains difficult: live benchmarks evaluate forecasts before answers exist, making them the cleanest way to measure forecasting ability, but they expire once events resolve; retrospective benchmarks are reproducible, but they cannot reliably distinguish genuine forecasting from facts a model may have already learned during pretraining. Prompting models to "pretend not to know" cannot replace a genuine knowledge boundary. We propose OracleProto, a reproducible framework for evaluating LLM native forecasting capability. OracleProto reconstructs resolved events into time-bounded forecasting samples by combining model-cutoff-aligned sample admission, tool-level temporal masking, content-level leakage detection, discrete answer normalization, and hierarchical scoring. Instantiated on a FutureX-Past-derived dataset with six contemporary LLMs, OracleProto distinguishes forecasting quality, sampling stability, and cost efficiency under controlled information boundaries, while reducing residual leakage to the $1\%$ level, an order of magnitude below tool-only temporal filtering. OracleProto turns LLM forecasting from one-off evaluation into an auditable, reusable, and trainable dataset-level capability, providing a unified interface for fair cross-model comparison and a controlled signal source for downstream SFT and RL. Code and data are available at https://github.com/MaYiding/OracleProto and https://huggingface.co/datasets/MaYiding/OracleProto.

</details>


### [127] [Agentic-imodels: Evolving agentic interpretability tools via autoresearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03808)
*Chandan Singh,Yan Shuo Tan,Weijia Xu,Zelalem Gero,Weiwei Yang,Michel Galley,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentic-imodels 是一种面向智能体的数据科学工具，通过优化预测性能和基于大语言模型（LLM）的可解释性指标，提升智能体对模型的理解与使用能力，在多个基准测试中显著提高了自动化数据科学系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据科学工具主要面向人类可读性设计，难以被智能体有效理解和利用。随着智能体在数据分析任务中的广泛应用，亟需开发专为智能体设计的、具备高可解释性的建模工具。

Method: 提出 Agentic-imodels 框架，构建一个可进化的自动研究循环，生成符合 scikit-learn 接口的回归模型库，并引入基于 LLM 的可解释性度量标准，评估智能体仅通过模型字符串表示即可模拟其行为的能力。

Result: 演化出的模型在预测性能和智能体可解释性方面均优于现有方法，并能泛化到新数据集和新的可解释性测试任务上；在 Copilot CLI、Claude Code 和 Codex 的 BLADE 基准测试中，端到端性能最高提升 73%。

Conclusion: Agentic-imodels 展示了为智能体定制数据科学工具的可行性与优势，推动了向全自动数据科学系统的演进。

Abstract: Agentic data science (ADS) systems are rapidly improving their capability to autonomously analyze, fit, and interpret data, potentially moving towards a future where agents conduct the vast majority of data-science work. However, current ADS systems use statistical tools designed to be interpretable by humans, rather than interpretable by agents. To address this, we introduce Agentic-imodels, an agentic autoresearch loop that evolves data-science tools designed to be interpretable by agents. Specifically, it develops a library of scikit-learn-compatible regressors for tabular data that are optimized for both predictive performance and a novel LLM-based interpretability metric. The metric measures a suite of LLM-graded tests that probe whether a fitted model's string representation is "simulatable" by an LLM, i.e. whether the LLM can answer questions about the model's behavior by reading its string output alone. We find that the evolved models jointly improve predictive performance and agent-facing interpretability, generalizing to new datasets and new interpretability tests. Furthermore, these evolved models improve downstream end-to-end ADS, increasing performance for Copilot CLI, Claude Code, and Codex on the BLADE benchmark by up to 73%

</details>


### [128] [Mechanical Conscience: A Mathematical Framework for Dependability of Machine Intelligenc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03847)
*Munkhdegerekh Batzorig,Purevbaatar Ganbold,Kyungbin Park,Pilkong Jeong,Kangbin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“机械良知”（Mechanical Conscience, MC）框架，用于在单智能体和分布式智能系统中实现轨迹层级的规范性监管，有效抑制因局部决策累积导致的全局不可接受行为，尤其适用于存在认知不确定性的边缘协同智能环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式协同智能（DCI）系统中，个体智能体的局部合理决策可能在不确定性下组合成全局不可接受的行为轨迹，而现有方法缺乏对行为轨迹层面的规范性评估与多参与者风险调控机制。

Method: 提出机械良知（MC）作为监督过滤器，通过最小化修正基线策略的动作，减少行为轨迹对规范可接受区域的累积偏离，并引入良知评分、机械罪责和共振可靠性等可解释概念；建立涵盖可接受性等价性、最优调节存在性与偏差单调递减的核心理论性质。

Result: 实验表明，MC调控下的智能体能维持轨迹层面的规范可接受性，而传统控制器则会偏离可接受边界；该框架还能自然扩展至多智能体DCI场景，抑制交互引发的涌现性风险。

Conclusion: 机械良知为分布式协同智能系统提供了一种可计算、可解释的轨迹级规范监管新范式，能够在保留自主性的同时保障全局行为的合规性与可靠性。

Abstract: Distributed collaborative intelligence (DCI), encompassing edge-to-edge architectures, federated learning, transfer learning, and swarm systems, creates environments in which emergent risk is structurally unavoidable: locally correct decisions by individual agents compose into globally unacceptable behavioral trajectories under uncertainty. Existing approaches such as constrained optimization, safe reinforcement learning, and runtime assurance evaluate acceptability at the level of individual actions rather than across behavioral trajectories, and none addresses the multi-participant, uncertainty-laden nature of DCI deployments. This paper introduces mechanical conscience (MC), a novel concept and simplified mathematical framework that operationalizes trajectory-level normative regulation for both single-agent and distributed intelligent systems. Mechanical conscience is defined as a supervisory filter that minimally corrects a baseline policy's actions to reduce cumulative deviation from a normatively admissible region, while accounting for epistemic uncertainty. We introduce associated constructs, conscience score, mechanical guilt, and resonant dependability, that provide an interpretable vocabulary and computable governance signals for this emerging field. Core theoretical properties are established: admissibility equivalence, existence of optimal regulation, and monotonic deviation reduction. Illustrative results demonstrate that MC-regulated agents maintain trajectory-level normative acceptability where conventional controllers drift outside admissible bounds, and that the framework naturally extends to suppress interaction-induced emergent risk in multi-agent DCI settings.

</details>


### [129] [Correct Is Not Enough: Training Reasoning Planners with Executor-Grounded Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03862)
*Tianyang Han,Hengyu Shi,Junjie Hu,Xu Yang,Zhiling Wang,Junhao Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TraceLift的规划-执行框架，通过将推理过程作为可消费的中间产物，并结合基于评分标准的奖励模型与执行器反馈，提升大语言模型中推理链的质量和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖最终答案正确性无法验证推理过程是否忠实、可靠或对模型有用，容易导致模型学习到看似合理但实际错误的推理路径。因此需要一种能同时评估推理质量和其对下游任务贡献的训练方法。

Method: 提出TraceLift框架：规划器生成带标签的推理链，固定执行器将其转化为最终输出并获得验证反馈；引入执行器锚定的奖励机制，将评分标准下的推理奖励模型得分与该推理对执行器性能提升的量化增益相乘，从而优化中间推理质量。构建TRACELIFT-GROUPS数据集，包含高质量参考推理与多种局部扰动的缺陷推理样例，用于训练和评估。

Result: 在代码和数学基准上的实验表明，相比仅依赖执行反馈的训练方式，TraceLift显著提升了两阶段系统的性能，验证了对推理过程进行质量监督的有效性。

Conclusion: 推理监督应不仅关注推理链表面质量，还需评估其对实际任务执行的帮助作用；TraceLift通过可验证奖励与结构化数据实现了更可靠的推理训练。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a common way to improve explicit reasoning in large language models, but final-answer correctness alone does not reveal whether the reasoning trace is faithful, reliable, or useful to the model that consumes it. This outcome-only signal can reinforce traces that are right for the wrong reasons, overstate reasoning gains by rewarding shortcuts, and propagate flawed intermediate states in multi-step systems. To this end, we propose TraceLift, a planner-executor training framework that treats reasoning as a consumable intermediate artifact. During planner training, the planner emits tagged reasoning. A frozen executor turns this reasoning into the final artifact for verifier feedback, while an executor-grounded reward shapes the intermediate trace. This reward multiplies a rubric-based Reasoning Reward Model (RM) score by measured uplift on the same frozen executor, crediting traces that are both high-quality and useful. To make reasoning quality directly learnable, we introduce TRACELIFT-GROUPS, a rubric-annotated reason-only dataset built from math and code seed problems. Each example is a same-problem group containing a high-quality reference trace and multiple plausible flawed traces with localized perturbations that reduce reasoning quality or solution support while preserving task relevance. Extensive experiments on code and math benchmarks show that this executor-grounded reasoning reward improves the two-stage planner-executor system over execution-only training, suggesting that reasoning supervision should evaluate not only whether a trace looks good, but also whether it helps the model that consumes it.

</details>


### [130] [EvoLM: Self-Evolving Language Models through Co-Evolved Discriminative Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03871)
*Shuyue Stella Li,Rui Xin,Teng Xiao,Yike Wang,Rulin Shao,Zoey Hao,Melanie Sclar,Sewoong Oh,Faeze Brahman,Pang Wei Koh,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVOLM是一种无需外部监督的后训练方法，通过将模型自身的评估能力结构化为判别性评分标准，实现语言模型的自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法依赖外部监督信号（如人工标注、专有模型或标量奖励模型），这些方法存在能力上限且难以扩展。而语言模型在预训练中已编码了丰富的评估知识，利用其自身评估能力作为奖励来源可突破限制，实现随模型能力共同增长的自我提升。

Method: EVOLM在单一语言模型内交替训练两种能力：(1) 评分生成器，生成针对实例的、以判别效用为目标优化的评估标准，使一个小型冻结裁判模型能更好地区分优劣响应；(2) 策略模型，使用基于生成评分的奖励信号进行训练。所有偏好信号均通过策略模型自身不同检查点输出的时间对比构建，无需人工标注或外部监督。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B模型上训练的EVOLM生成的评分标准在RewardBench-2上比GPT-4.1高出25.7%；联合训练的策略模型在OLMo3-Adapt套件上平均得分为69.3%，优于使用GPT-4.1提示生成评分训练的策略3.9%，优于当前最先进的8B奖励模型SkyWork-RM达16%。

Conclusion: EVOLM表明，将模型的评估能力结构化为协同演化的判别性评分标准，能够在无需外部监督的情况下实现有效的自我改进，为语言模型的自主进化提供了新路径。

Abstract: Language models encode substantial evaluative knowledge from pretraining, yet current post-training methods rely on external supervision (human annotations, proprietary models, or scalar reward models) to produce reward signals. Each imposes a ceiling. Human judgment cannot supervise capabilities beyond its own, proprietary APIs create dependencies, and verifiable rewards cover only domains with ground-truth answers. Self-improvement from a model's own evaluative capacity is a reward source that scales with the model itself, yet remains largely untapped by current methods. We introduce EVOLM, a post-training method that structures this capacity into explicit discriminative rubrics and uses them as training signal. EVOLM trains two capabilities within a single language model in alternation: (1) a rubric generator producing instance-specific evaluation criteria optimized for discriminative utility, which maximizes a small frozen judge's ability to distinguish preferred from dispreferred responses; and (2) a policy trained using those rubric-conditioned scores as reward. All preference signals are constructed from the policy's own outputs via temporal contrast with earlier checkpoints, requiring no human annotation or external supervision. EVOLM trains a Qwen3-8B model to generate rubrics that outperform GPT-4.1 on RewardBench-2 by 25.7%. The co-trained policy achieves 69.3% average on the OLMo3-Adapt suite, outperforming policies trained with GPT-4.1 prompted rubrics by 3.9% and with the state-of-the-art 8B reward model SkyWork-RM by 16%. Overall, EVOLM demonstrates that structuring a model's evaluative capacity into co-evolving discriminative rubrics enables self-improvement without external supervision.

</details>


### [131] [From Intent to Execution: Composing Agentic Workflows with Agent Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03986)
*Kishan Athrey,Ramin Pishehvar,Brian Riordan,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自动化构建多智能体系统（MAS）的框架，通过集成LLM驱动的规划器、动态调用图、任务分配协调器和基于两阶段信息检索的智能体推荐器，实现了从任务描述到系统执行的全自动流程。实验表明该框架在召回率、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面优于现有方法，并验证了批判性智能体在提升系统性能中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统构建依赖大量人工参与，包括手动规划、智能体选择和执行图构建，效率低且难以扩展，因此需要一种自动化框架来提升开发效率与系统性能。

Method: 提出一个包含LLM衍生规划器、自然语言任务描述、动态调用图、协调器和两阶段检索机制（快速检索器+LLM重排序器）的智能体推荐系统的框架，并引入一个全局审视计划的批判性智能体进行整体评估与优化。

Result: 端到端实验结果显示该框架在召回率上优于当前最先进方法，具备更强的鲁棒性和可扩展性；加入批判性智能体后进一步提升了召回分数。

Conclusion: 自动化构建多智能体系统是可行且高效的，综合性的任务驱动智能体选择与审查机制是构建高性能MAS的关键组成部分。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built using AI agents fulfill a variety of user intents that may be used to design and build a family of related applications. However, the creation of such MAS currently involves manual composition of the plan, manual selection of appropriate agents, and manual creation of execution graphs. This paper introduces a framework for the automated creation of multi-agent systems which replaces multiple manual steps with an automated framework. The proposed framework consists of software modules and a workflow to orchestrate the requisite task- specific application. The modules include: an LLM-derived planner, a set of tasks described in natural language, a dynamic call graph, an orchestrator for map agents to tasks, and an agent recommender that finds the most suitable agent(s) from local and global agent registries. The agent recommender uses a two-stage information retrieval (IR) system comprising a fast retriever and an LLM-based re-ranker. We implemented a series of experiments exploring the choice of embedders, re- rankers, agent description enrichment, and supervising critique agent. We benchmarked this system end-to-end, evaluating the combination of planning, agent selection, and task completion, with our proposed approach. Our experimental results show that our approach outperforms the state-of-the- art in terms of the recall rate and is more robust and scalable compared to previous approaches. The critique agent holistically reevaluates both agent and tool recommendations against the overall plan. We show that the inclusion of the critique agent further enhances the recall score, proving that the comprehensive review and revision of task-based agent selection is an essential step in building end-to-end multi-agent systems.

</details>
